IV. <em>pains</em> and<br><em>ahars</em> of Bengal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-367">325</a>; area irrigated<br>by private canals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-368">326</a>; great public<br>canals: inundation, mostly in Indus<br>Valley, dependent upon high or flood<br>water of rivers, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-369">327</a>; area of irrigation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-59">17</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-60">18</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-369">327</a>; perennial, with or<br>without a weir or ‘anicut,’ 326; canals<br>made before British rule, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-369">327</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-370">328</a>;<br>failure of private enterprise to construct large irrigation works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-370">328</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-371">329</a>;<br>new works undertaken by the state,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-371">329</a>; ‘Major’ works, with borrowed<br>capital, productive or remunerative,<br>‘protective’ against famine, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-371">329</a>; include storage or tanks, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-372">330</a>; ‘Minor’<br>works, paid for out of general revenues,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-372">330</a>; a table of capital expended, area<br>irrigated, and of revenue returned upon<br>each major work and upon total minor<br>works in each Province, with totals for<br>each and all, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-373">331</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-374">332</a>; principal<br>works in the several Provinces, 330–<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-387">345</a>; in Punjab and Frontier Province,<br>major, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-372">330</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-375">333</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-376">334</a>, minor, including <button type="button" class="folio-trigger folio-turn" id="page-547" data-page="547" data-folio="505" data-scan="https://archive.org/download/imperialgazette03hunt/page/n546_w1400.jpg" aria-label="Inspect original scan for page 505">[p. 505]</button> inundation canals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-376">334</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-377">335</a>, scope for extension, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-377">335</a>; in Sind, its entire dependence upon irrigation, major and minor works, mainly inundation canals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-377">335</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-378">336</a>; in Bombay, Deccan and Gujarāt, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-379">337</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-380">338</a>; Lakes Fife and Whiting, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-379">337</a>; supply by Muthā Canal of waterworks of Poona and Kirkee, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-379">337</a>; reasons for their financial failure, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-379">337</a>; further extension urgently needed in Deccan, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-380">338</a>; in Madras: major and minor works, the three deltaic systems, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-380">338</a>, storage works, the Periyār project, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-381">339</a>, need of extension of ‘protective’ works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-382">340</a>, Sir Arthur Cotton’s Tungabhadra project, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-382">340</a>; in Bengal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-382">340</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-383">341</a>; in United Provinces: the Ganges Canal, the Lower Ganges Canal, the Agra Canal, all remunerative, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-383">341</a>, the ‘protective’ Betwā Canal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-383">341</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-384">342</a>; small scope for extension in United Provinces, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-384">342</a>; its urgent need in districts south of the Jumna, its difficulties, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-384">342</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-385">343</a>; in Ajmer-Merwāra, minor works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-385">343</a>, tanks and wells the only source of irrigation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-385">343</a>; in Baluchistān, minor works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-385">343</a>, private works numerous, the <em>kārez</em> or underground tunnel, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-385">343</a>; in Upper Burma, the Mandalay and Shwebo Canals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-385">343</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-386">344</a>; numerous private works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-386">344</a>; in Central Provinces and Berār, state works contemplated, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-386">344</a>; total areas irrigated from state and private canals in British India, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-387">345</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-388">346</a>; (1) which are also irrigation works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-397">355</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-400">358</a>; Godāvari and Kistna Canals, traffic large and beneficial, but not directly remunerative, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-397">355</a>; Orissa Canals, Midnapore, and Son Canals, traffic moderate, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-398">356</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-399">357</a>; in Madras, Buckingham Canal, of public utility and moderate traffic, and others, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-400">358</a>; indirect benefits, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-402">360</a>; outside deltaic tract canals of small use or value as means of inland communication, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-402">360</a>; the Sātpurā<br>group of fields, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-176">134</a>; the Mohpāni field,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-177">135</a>; outside the<br>Peninsula at Darjeeling, owing to the<br>geological folding, coal crushed, poor,<br>and hard to work, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-178">136</a>; younger coals<br>of Cretaceous and Tertiary age, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-178">136</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-180">138</a>;<br>a table of outputs, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-179">137</a>; compared<br>with Gondwāna coals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-178">136</a>; fields in<br>NE. Internal trade, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-343">301</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-348">306</a>.<br>Bibliography, p. <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-348">306</a>. its extensive domestic use and saleability, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-279">237</a>;<br>rise and fall of copper traffic a barometer<br>of pecuniary condition of the people,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-279">237</a>; held to be the purest of metals,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-274">232</a>; has to be tinned for cooking,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-279">237</a>; fall in imports during famine<br>years, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-279">237</a>; copper and brass wares,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-282">240</a>, their graceful shapes, the <em>lotā</em> of<br>the Hindus, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-282">240</a>, the spouted <em>tonti</em><br>of Muhammadans, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-283">241</a>; artistic brass-work of Jaipur, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-283">241</a>; principal centres<br>of the industry, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-283">241</a>; variations in<br>wholesale prices of imported copper,<br>1861-1903, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-504">462</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Corundum, an ancient abrasive, its gradual<br>disuse, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-193">151</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Cotton, Sir Arthur, his work on the<br>irrigation systems of the deltas of the<br>Cauvery, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-369">327</a>, of the Godāvari and the</p><div class="page-rail-marker" id="page-549"><button type="button" class="folio-trigger" data-page="549" data-folio="507" data-scan="https://archive.org/download/imperialgazette03hunt/page/n548_w1400.jpg" aria-label="Inspect scan for page 507">[p. 507]</button></div><p class="index-entry">Kistna, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-380">338</a>; exports,<br>mainly of twist and yarn, to China,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-324">282</a>; variations from 1861 to 1903<br>in wholesale prices of imports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-504">462</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Cotton, raw, its cultivation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-84">42</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-88">46</a>; mode of cultivation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-88">46</a>; out-turn, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-88">46</a>; exports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-90">48</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-328">286</a>; its competition with American, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-328">286</a>; recent market<br>in Japan, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-328">286</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Cotton industries, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-237">195</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-245">203</a>; cloths, of N. India,<br>of United Provinces, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-241">199</a>, of Central<br>Provinces and Berār, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-241">199</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-242">200</a>, of Bengal, of Bombay Presidency, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-242">200</a>, of<br>Madras Presidency, of Mysore and<br>Burma, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-242">200</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-243">201</a>; muslins, plain and<br>figured, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-243">201</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-244">202</a>; of Dacca, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-243">201</a>, <em>jāmdānis</em> of Bengal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-243">201</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-244">202</a>, of United<br>Provinces, of Rājputāna and Central<br>India, of Madras, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-244">202</a>; twist and yarn,<br>production of and improvement of,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-244">202</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-245">203</a>, exports of, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-245">203</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-324">282</a>; woven<br>piece-goods, &amp;c., production of, white,<br>grey, and coloured, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-245">203</a>; cotton carpets and rugs, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-259">217</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Credit, agricultural, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-132">90</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-134">92</a>; the land<br>worked on borrowed capital, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-132">90</a>; evil<br>results of excessive indebtedness of peasant, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-58">16</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-132">90</a>; wide credit due to security<br>of British rule, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-132">90</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-133">91</a>; high rates of<br>interest due to competition for loans,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-132">90</a>; frequent sales or foreclosures, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-132">90</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-133">91</a>; curtailment by Government of<br>peasants’ right to raise money on land,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-133">91</a>, and institution of loans at moderate<br>rates, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-133">91</a>; Act to encourage co-operative credit on the Raffeisen plan, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-133">91</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Crops, mixed, intermittent, in rotation,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-66">24</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-67">25</a>; principal crops, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-69">27</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-118">76</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-140">98</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-142">100</a>; proportion of cropped land,<br>of cultivable, waste, and forests, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-45">3</a>.<br><em>See also</em> Cultivation, Soils.</p><p class="index-entry">Cultivation or tilth of the soil, good and<br>bad, as elsewhere, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-48">6</a>; best in the Peninsula, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-49">7</a>; checked by want of capital, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-49">7</a>;<br>its implements, few, simple, indigenous,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-53">11</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-57">15</a>; ploughs, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-54">12</a>; scarifiers and seed-drills for Deccan trap, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-55">13</a>; bullock-hoes,<br>harrows, levellers, and clod-crushers,<br>carts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-56">14</a>; hand-tools: crowbars, <em>kodālis</em> or <em>māmūtis</em> (used for spade work),<br>picks, sickles, winnowing sieves, and<br>scoops (<em>sūp</em>), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-56">14</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-57">15</a>; methods of tilth,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-57">15</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-58">16</a>; its various methods and skill,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-48">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-49">7</a>; defective selection of seeds, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-58">16</a>;<br>irrigation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-58">16</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>, its paramount necessity, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-58">16</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-134">92</a>, its applicability to<br>different soils, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-59">17</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-61">19</a>; high farming<br>in Peninsula under well-irrigation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-61">19</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>; manures, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>; fallowing, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>;<br>intermittent cultivation in virgin and<br>reclaimed soils, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-66">24</a>, its method in Western Ghāts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-66">24</a>; shifting cultivation by<br>aboriginal hill tribes, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-66">24</a>, its waste and<br>dangers of fire, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-67">25</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-147">105</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-156">114</a>, its control, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-67">25</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-156">114</a>; rotation of crops, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-67">25</a>;<br>mixed crops as a substitute and insurance, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-67">25</a>; advantages of sowing<br>pulses with cereals, especially <em>arhar</em>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-67">25</a>. <em>See also</em> Labour, Irrigation, Soils.</p><p class="index-entry">Customs, inland, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-305">263</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-306">264</a>; their imposition, and their irregularity, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-306">264</a>;<br>on salt and sugar, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-306">264</a>; their reduction, unification, and practical abandonment in the British Provinces, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-306">264</a>;<br>still maintained by some of the Native<br>States, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-306">264</a>; municipal octrois, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-306">264</a>.<br><em>See also</em> Vol. IV, ch. ix.</p><p class="index-entry">Customs, sea, on imports and exports,<br>their imposition and reduction, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-305">263</a>.<br><em>See also</em> Vol. IV, chap. viii.</p><p class="index-entry">Cutch, catechu, and gambier, resinous extracts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-213">171</a>; increase<br>in butter-making, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-125">83</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-126">84</a>; exports and<br>imports of butter, <em>ghī</em>, and condensed<br>milk, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-126">84</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Dalhousie, Lord, inaugurated a permanent forest policy, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-149">107</a>; class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-263">221</a>, other great centres,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-263">221</a>; lace and network of S. India,<br>patchwork of Kashmīr, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-259">217</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-264">222</a>;<br><em>kalagas</em> of Burma, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-263">221</a>; gold and silver<br>wire embroidery, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-263">221</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-264">222</a>; <em>zardozī</em>,<br>heavy and gorgeous for state purposes,<br>of earliest history, its modern centres of<br>production, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-264">222</a>; <em>kāmdāni</em>, light and<br>graceful, a variety of <em>chikan</em>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-263">221</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-264">222</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Enamelling, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-280">238</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-281">239</a>; its three methods:<br>practised principally in Kashmīr and<br>Jaipur, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-280">238</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-281">239</a>; niello, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-281">239</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Engines, railway, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-427">385</a>; average loads on<br>best railways, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-427">385</a>; barely half of best</p><p class="index-entry">American, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-427">385</a>; necessity for best results<br>of strengthening bridges and roads, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-427">385</a>;<br>move towards their complete construction in India, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-429">387</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Exports and Imports. <em>See</em> Imports and<br>Exports.</p><h2 class="sec-h1">F.</h2><p class="index-entry">Factories, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-288">246</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>; legislation for their<br>regulation from 1879 to 1901, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-288">246</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>; regulation of hours of labour<br>and of sanitary matters, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-288">246</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>;<br>their inspection, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>; a table of factories<br>and hands employed in 1893 and 1903,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>. <em>See also</em> Mills.</p><p class="index-entry">Famine, areas of liability and immunity,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-47">5</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-48">6</a>; causes of, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-517">475</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>; a disease of<br>all purely agricultural countries, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-517">475</a>;<br>peculiarly so of India, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-518">476</a>; its dependence upon a rainfall periodic not regular, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-47">5</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-358">316</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-518">476</a>; the monsoons and their<br>harvests, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-518">476</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>; chain of deficient<br>rainfall, failure of crops, rise in prices,<br>fall in demand for labour and in wages,<br>pestilence, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-536">494</a>; removal of former<br>checks on population, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>; subletting<br>or subdivision of small holdings, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>;<br>competition rents and low wages, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>;<br>alleged extension of area of distress by<br>railways equalizing prices and discouraging storage, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-538">496</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-539">497</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Famine, modern relief policy, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-519">477</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-525">483</a>;<br>its principles laid down in 1877, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-520">478</a>,<br>accepted by three Famine Commissions,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-520">478</a>; its practical difficulties, of huge<br>numbers, extended areas, various castes,<br>creeds, and languages, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-521">479</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-525">483</a>; a<br>modern plan of campaign, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-521">479</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>;<br>standing preparations, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-521">479</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-522">480</a>; danger<br>signals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-522">480</a>; preliminary action, ‘moral<br>strategy,’ 480; the period of test-works,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-522">480</a>; the period of general relief, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-523">481</a>;<br>at maximum of distress in May, frequent<br>outbreaks of cholera, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-523">481</a>; heroism of<br>English officers during Gujarāt outbreak in 1900, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-523">481</a>; efficient management and stay of outbreak in 1897 in<br>Bundelkhand, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-523">481</a>; change of policy<br>during the rains, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-523">481</a>; recall of labour<br>to the land, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>; closure of relief by<br>middle of October, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>; distribution<br>of quinine, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>; charitable relief funds,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>; the Indian People’s Famine Trust<br>founded by the Mahārājā of Jaipur,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>; improved communications and<br>greater knowledge the main causes of<br>efficiency in famine relief, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-524">482</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-525">483</a>;<br>present elasticity in the relief system,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-525">483</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Famine, prevention of, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-537">495</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-541">499</a>; by more<br>accurate knowledge of country and<br>people, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-537">495</a>; by productive and protective railways and irrigation works, <button type="button" class="folio-trigger folio-turn" id="page-551" data-page="551" data-folio="509" data-scan="https://archive.org/download/imperialgazette03hunt/page/n550_w1400.jpg" aria-label="Inspect original scan for page 509">[p. 509]</button> <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-537">495</a>; by Famine Relief and Insurance<br>Grant, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-371">329</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-412">370</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-413">371</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-534">492</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-537">495</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-538">496</a>;<br>by other efforts to increase material<br>prosperity, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-539">497</a>; evidence of success in<br>recuperative power of the country, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-539">497</a>,<br>increase of cropped areas before, during,<br>and after famines of 1897 and 1900,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-540">498</a>, in steady growth of general<br>prosperity, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-540">498</a>, the one exception in<br>agricultural labour, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-541">499</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Famine Insurance Grant, its institution,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-412">370</a>; a portion of it devoted to construction of protective railways, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-413">371</a>,<br>and irrigation works, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-371">329</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-537">495</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-538">496</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Famines, history of chief, and of relief, 483-<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-537">495</a>; under native rule famines frequent<br>and frightful, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-517">475</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-526">484</a>, relief spasmodic<br>and ineffectual, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-517">475</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-520">478</a>; under East<br>India Company, 1770 to 1858, 483-<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-527">485</a>, empirical policy, attempts to fix<br>prices of grain, prohibition of export,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-526">484</a>; relief works instituted in 1792,<br>famine wage first in 1832; remissions of<br>revenue, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-526">484</a>; under the Crown to 1880,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-527">485</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-531">489</a>; acceptance of state responsibility and a more humane policy, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-527">485</a>;<br>famine of 1860-1, opening of poorhouses, relief to ladies at home, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-527">485</a>;<br>Col. Baird-Smith’s inquiry and report,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-527">485</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-528">486</a>; the Orissa famine of 1865-7,<br>its management criticized by Sir George<br>Campbell’s Commission and by Famine<br>Commission of 1880, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-528">486</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-529">487</a>; Rājputānā famine of 1868-70, aggravated<br>by cholera, locusts, and fever, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-529">487</a>,<br>advances to cultivators, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-529">487</a>; Bihār<br>famine of 1873-4, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-530">488</a>; life to be saved<br>at any cost, enormous and lax expenditure, village inspection, advance in<br>administration, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-530">488</a>; S. I. Indian Mines<br>Act, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-208">166</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-209">167</a>; factory legislation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-288">246</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>; table of factories and hands, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-289">247</a>;<br>statistics of rural, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-290">248</a>, and urban labour<br>in different districts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-290">248</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-291">249</a>; of unskilled labour, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-291">249</a>; of labour engaged<br>in undertakings founded with European<br>capital or conducted on European<br>methods, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-292">250</a>; tables of persons employed in or supported by various<br>occupations and industries, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-292">250</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-293">251</a>;<br>wages of labour, skilled and unskilled,<br>between 1873 and 1903, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-508">466</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-516">474</a>; agricultural labour the one exception to<br>general increase of prosperity, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-541">499</a>. <em>See</em><br><em>also</em> Wages.</p><p class="index-entry">Lac, a resinous extract, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-214">172</a>-<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>, yields<br>a dye and a resin, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-214">172</a>; production by<br>insect, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-215">173</a>; collection and manufacture, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-215">173</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-216">174</a>; its numerous industrial uses, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-216">174</a>; artistic work, lac<br>turnery, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-216">174</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>; factories and trade,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-216">174</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>; decline of exports of lac dye,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-226">184</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-333">291</a>; lac-coloured metal at<br>Morādābād and Jaipur, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-279">237</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-280">238</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Lacquer-work, Burmese, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>; its chief<br>material, <em>thitsī</em>, a vegetable oleo-resin<br>(not lac), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>; its industrial applications, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>; Burmese glass mosaic work,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-218">176</a>; trade, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-218">176</a>. <button type="button" class="folio-trigger folio-turn" id="page-554" data-page="554" data-folio="512" data-scan="https://archive.org/download/imperialgazette03hunt/page/n553_w1400.jpg" aria-label="Inspect original scan for page 512">[p. 512]</button></p><p class="index-entry">Land and Landlords. <em>See</em> Rent, Labour,<br>Tenures.</p><p class="index-entry">Lapidary work, jade, agate, garnets, rock-crystal, turquoise, glassware of Patna,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-285">243</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Lead (galena), its wide distribution<br>associated with silver and zinc, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-187">145</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Leather, manufacture of, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-231">189</a>; tanneries,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-231">189</a>; leather work, boots and shoes,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-232">190</a>, artistic manufactures, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-232">190</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-233">191</a>;<br>imports and exports of boots and shoes,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-232">190</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Limes and cements for building, <em>kankar</em>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-192">150</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Linseed. <em>See</em> Oilseeds.</p><h2 class="sec-h1">M.</h2><p class="index-entry">Madras, port of, its exposed shore and<br>poor harbour, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-317">275</a>; depression by railways of import and export trade, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-317">275</a>;<br>its imports and exports from 1882 to<br>1902, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-314">272</a>; internal trade with Madras<br>ports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-345">303</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Magnesite, mines near Salem, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-196">154</a>; for<br>bricks to line furnaces, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-196">154</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Manganese, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-188">146</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-189">147</a>; rapid growth of mining and quarrying industry, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-188">146</a>; table<br>of output from 1899 to 1903, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-188">146</a>; its<br>mode of occurrence, and dimensions of<br>ore bodies, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-188">146</a>; composition of Nāgpur ore, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-188">146</a>; distribution of the ore,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-189">147</a>; statistics of labour employed in<br>industry, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-205">163</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Mango and <em>Mahuā</em>, their great use as<br>food, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-118">76</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Manure, next to water the ryot’s need, 20-<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>; waste of urine, use of dung as fuel,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>; town waste as manure, use of<br>sullage, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>; sewage farms, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-63">21</a>; village manure generally wasted, exhaustion of soil, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-63">21</a>; need of improved<br>methods, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-63">21</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>; their adoption in<br>some parts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-63">21</a>; green manures, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-63">21</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-147">105</a>; mineral manures, native and imported, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-64">22</a>; bones as manure, prejudice<br>against, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-64">22</a>; exported, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-64">22</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-197">155</a>; oil-cakes and fish manure, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-64">22</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>; ploughing in of green crops, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-65">23</a>; small imports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-198">156</a>.</p><p class="index-entry"><em>Mashrū</em> (‘permitted’), a textile, usually<br>of mixed cotton and silk, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-229">187</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-253">211</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Meteorological division of India into<br>Northern, between Himālayas and<br>Vindhyas, Peninsular, south of Vindhyas, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-45">3</a>; their relations to the monsoons, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-46">4</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-48">6</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Mica, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-194">152</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-196">154</a>; areas of its production,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-195">153</a>; primitive methods of mining, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-195">153</a>;<br>large profits from, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-195">153</a>; output, half<br>the world’s supply, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-194">152</a>; exports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-194">152</a>;<br>exports of films for micanite, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-195">153</a>; statistics of labour employed in mines,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-205">163</a>, and death-rate, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-208">166</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Millets, one of the staple foods, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-266">224</a>;<br>areas of production, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-74">32</a>; <em>jowār</em> (in<br>Madras, <em>cholam</em>) or grey millet, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-74">32</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-76">34</a>;<br>its varieties, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-74">32</a>; its soils and cultivation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-75">33</a>; tillage and sowing, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-75">33</a>; harvesting, threshing, and sifting, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-76">34</a>; a<br><em>kharīf</em> and <em>rabi</em> crop, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-75">33</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-76">34</a>; in rotation with cotton, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-75">33</a>; its importance as<br>a fodder, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-74">32</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-75">33</a>; <em>bājra</em> (in Madras,<br><em>cambu</em>), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-74">32</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-76">34</a>; consumed mainly at<br>home, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-76">34</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-266">224</a>; exports slight, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-76">34</a>; retail<br>prices of <em>jowār</em> and <em>bājra</em> from 1861 to<br>1903, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-500">458</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Mills, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-268">226</a>; in pre-British times only<br>trade routes and caravan tracks, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-444">402</a>;<br>principal routes, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-445">403</a>, marked by pillars,<br>guarded by posts, maintained by tolls,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-445">403</a>; tolls<br>rare and chiefly at ferries and on hill<br>roads, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-451">409</a>;
