creation of different Departments to deal with Agriculture, Commerce and Industry, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-309">267</a>; Chambers<br>of Commerce, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-309">267</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-310">268</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Traffic: difficulties of early, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-303">261</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-444">402</a>;<br>on railways, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-427">385</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-429">387</a>; passenger: enormous development of, due to pilgrimages, religious festivals, and migrations<br>of labour, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-427">385</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-428">386</a>; 200 millions of<br>third-class passengers in 1904 carried<br>at less than a farthing a mile, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-428">386</a>;<br>goods: development of, even greater<br>than of passenger, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-428">386</a>; creation of new<br>markets and equalization of prices, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-428">386</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/14-chapter-x-famine/#page-538">496</a>; transport in 1904 of 52 millions<br>of tons at less than a halfpenny per<br>ton per mile, each ton averaging 172<br>miles, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-428">386</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-429">387</a>; chief articles of traffic,<br>grain and seeds, coal, cotton, jute, salt,<br>sugar, and timber, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-428">386</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-429">387</a>; development of local products and wealth<br>a cause of more imports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-429">387</a>; on<br>navigable canals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-396">354</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-402">360</a>, not one<br>directly renumerative, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-401">359</a>. <em>See also</em><br>Tables IV–VI, pp. <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-454">412</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/11-chapter-vii-railways-and-roads/#page-455">413</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Trees of the forests, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-144">102</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-146">104</a>; protection<br>of, from fire, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-155">113</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-157">115</a>, from cattle, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-158">116</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Tungsten and Titanium ores, found but<br>not worked, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-190">148</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Turmeric, a dye and a condiment, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-225">183</a>, its<br>extensive cultivation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-225">183</a>; decline of<br>exports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-225">183</a>.</p><h2 class="sec-h1">V.</h2><p class="index-entry">Varnishes, of lac, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-216">174</a>, of <em>thitsī</em>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-217">175</a>, of<br>other resins, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-218">176</a>; used in gesso work,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-218">176</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Vegetables, garden, a list of principal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-117">75</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Village industries in metal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-274">232</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-276">234</a>;<br>metal often in place of glass and<br>porcelain of Europe, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-274">232</a>; local industries often on a large scale and of<br>more than local fame, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-276">234</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Voelcker, Dr., his <em>Report on the Improvement of Indian Agriculture</em>, quoted,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-48">6</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-58">16</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-62">20</a>.</p><h2 class="sec-h1">W.</h2><p class="index-entry">Wages, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-508">466</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-516">474</a>; official data only from<br>1873, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-508">466</a>; a general rise save in Oudh,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-514">472</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-516">474</a>; data untrustworthy for exact<br>comparisons, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-509">467</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-510">468</a>; distinction<br>drawn between skilled (artisan) and<br>unskilled (agricultural and domestic)<br>labour, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-508">466</a>; in rural areas wages<br>generally paid in kind with perquisites,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-509">467</a>; irregularity of employment, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-509">467</a>;<br>cash payments in urban areas, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-510">468</a>;<br>variations in wages in different localities<br>and under different circumstances, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-510">468</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-511">469</a>; wages lowest in agricultural and<br>congested districts, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-510">468</a>, highest in<br>industrial centres, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-511">469</a>, varying with<br>demand and supply of labour, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-511">469</a>;<br>relation between cost of living and<br>wages illustrated by rise in wages and<br>in price of rice and food in Bengal,<br>Assam, and Punjab, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-511">469</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-512">470</a>; greater<br>rise in wages of skilled labour not<br>generally apparent, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-512">470</a>, depression of <button type="button" class="folio-trigger folio-turn" id="page-562" data-page="562" data-folio="520" data-scan="https://archive.org/download/imperialgazette03hunt/page/n561_w1400.jpg" aria-label="Inspect original scan for page 520">[p. 520]</button> wages by immobility of labour and low<br>standards of comfort, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-512">470</a>; general improvement in these respects, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-512">470</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-513">471</a>;<br>migration of labour, <em>see</em> Vol. I, ch. ix;<br>evidence of material progress in consumption of salt, excise revenue, and<br>savings banks, 471; famine wage, 490,<br>492, 494. Tables of average monthly<br>wages in different Provinces from 1873<br>to 1903, with percentage of rise or fall:<br>I. of agricultural labourer, 472; II.<br>of syce or horse-keeper, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-515">473</a>; III. of<br>common mason, carpenter, or blacksmith, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-516">474</a>; wages in coal mines, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/07-chapter-iii-mines-and-minerals/#page-207">165</a>.<br>Bibliography of Prices and Wages, p. <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-513">471</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Waste cultivable land, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-44">2</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-45">3</a>, proportion of,<br>to cultivated area and to forests, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-45">3</a>;<br>still enough land for the whole rural<br>population, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-44">2</a>; its reclamation by irrigation and colonization; in Punjab, the<br>Rechna Doāb and other areas, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-376">334</a>,<br>possibilities of extension, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-377">335</a>; in Sind,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-377">335</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-378">336</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Water-supply, influence of forests as<br>affording storage, regulation of, and protection against excessive rainfalls, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/06-chapter-ii-forests/#page-146">104</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Wax, bees’, its area of production, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-218">176</a>;<br>its uses: employed in dyeing, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-219">177</a>; its<br>export, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-218">176</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Wells, irrigation by, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-360">318</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-364">322</a>; the work<br>of private enterprise, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-360">318</a>; area supplied<br>by, and produce, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-360">318</a>; temporary and<br>permanent, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-361">319</a>, their cost, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-361">319</a>; lifting<br>appliances, by manual or bullock<br>power, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-361">319</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-362">320</a>; conditions favourable<br>to irrigation by wells, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-360">318</a>; state encouragement of, by terminable loans<br>(<em>takāvi</em>), <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-362">320</a>; or by remission of land<br>revenue, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-363">321</a>; its extension during<br>recent years, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-363">321</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-364">322</a>; most extensively practised in the United Provinces,<br>and the Provinces of Punjab, Bombay,<br>Madras, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-61">19</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-363">321</a>; total area in British<br>India irrigated from wells, public and<br>private, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-387">345</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/10-chapter-vi-irrigation-and-navigation/#page-388">346</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Wheat; its cultivation, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-71">29</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-73">31</a>; area of<br>production, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-72">30</a>; varieties, ‘dry’ crop, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-72">30</a>,<br>irrigated, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-72">30</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-73">31</a>; harvesting and threshing, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-73">31</a>; out-turn, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-73">31</a>; exports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-73">31</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-326">284</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-327">285</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-501">459</a>; not the staple food, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-266">224</a>;<br>statistics of retail prices of, from 1861<br>to 1903, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-500">458</a>; variations in prices of<br>wholesale exports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-505">463</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-506">464</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Wines, white and red of Kashmīr, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-267">225</a>;<br>compete in purity and excellence with<br>French, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-267">225</a>; consumption, mainly of<br>imported, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-267">225</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Wood-carving, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-270">228</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>; as applied to<br>architecture, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-270">228</a>, in N. India, mostly<br>a Muhammadan growth, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-271">229</a>, its<br>adaptation to <em>deodār</em>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-271">229</a>, its application to doors, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-271">229</a>, its important centres,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-271">229</a>; in the United and Central Provinces, its character and centres, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-271">229</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>; its place taken by stone-carving<br>in Rājputāna, Central India, Sind,<br>Baluchistān, and Bengal, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>; in<br>Gujarāt, a Jain type, and an evolved<br>Muhammadan, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>; of Khāndesh and<br>Deccan, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>; in S. India, sandal-wood<br>carving of Mysore reproduces characteristics of its Chālukyan temples, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>,<br>wood-carving of Madras those of<br>Dravidian temples, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>; in Burma,<br>its great development owing to absence<br>of masonry and supply of teak, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>, its wooden statuary, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>; in<br>Nepāl, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>; sandal-wood carving, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>;<br>as applied to furniture, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-271">229</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-272">230</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Woodwork, inlaid, with metals, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>;<br>painted, at Sāvantvādi, of furniture,<br>caskets, and shrines, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>, at Muzaffargarh, of bows and arrows, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-273">231</a>, at<br>Jhānsi and Gwalior, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-274">232</a>.</p><p class="index-entry">Wool, industry and trade, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-254">212</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-260">218</a>; inferior quality of, from native sheep, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-128">86</a>,<br><a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/05-chapter-i-agriculture/#page-129">87</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-254">212</a>; centres of production, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-255">213</a>;<br>manufactures and mills and hand-loom<br>factories, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-255">213</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>; capital and labour<br>engaged, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-255">213</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>; total production in<br>1903, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>; Australian wool imported<br>for finer goods, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>; goods manufactured, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-260">218</a>; pile carpets, originally<br>of good quality and design, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>,<br>deterioration by low prices for European<br>market, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>, recent recovery, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-256">214</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-257">215</a>;<br>carpet-weaving in N. brocaded woollen piece-goods,<br><em>chogas, jāmawār</em>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-260">218</a>; trade, raw<br>wool, imports and exports, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-260">218</a>, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/09-chapter-v-commerce-and-trade/#page-333">291</a>;<br>manufactured wool, increase in imports;<br>decrease in exports, save of carpets and<br>shawls, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/08-chapter-iv-arts-and-manufactures/#page-260">218</a>. <em>See also Pashm.</em></p><h2 class="sec-h1">Z.</h2><p class="index-entry">Zamīndāri tracts or areas, assessment and<br>collection of land revenue in, <a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-489">447</a>;<br>protection of rights of ‘occupants’ and<br>‘non-occupants,’ 449–<a class="index-folio-link" href="/books/imperial-gazetteer-of-india-vol-03-economic/chapters/13-chapter-ix-rents-prices-and-wages/#page-493">451</a>. <em>See</em> Rent.</p><div class="prose-unit"><p class="imprint-line">Oxford: Printed at the Clarendon Press by <span class="sc">Horace Hart, M.A.</span></p></div>
